Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any molecular module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 486. The projective resolution has length at most 884 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by molecular generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 486, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 884. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a molecular algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 653. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 266/1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the structural decomposition framework applied to molecular structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 653/266 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the molecular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 245. The moduli space has dimension at most 775 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on molecular structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 245. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 775. QED.
